The finding says `TarballWithheldError` already carries the `Response`; it keeps only `status` and `statusText`, folded into its message. Discovery only. Open.
- `2026-10-23-the-retired-folder-notice-says-move-it-onto-a-folder-that-already-holds-the-live-installation` — `retiredSourceFolderUnsupported` always ends "Move it to `<root>/.agents-inc/claude/`", and nothing asks whether that folder exists. When it does, it holds the installation the CLI is reading: a literal `mv` nests the retired folder inside it as `.agents-inc/claude/.claude-src/`, which silences the notice and migrates nothing, and moving the files one by one overwrites the live `config.ts` (reproduced with a throwaway HOME holding both). No spec puts both folders in one root. This is the half `2026-10-21-the-retired-folder-notice-names-no-root-so-a-home-only-folder-reads-as-the-projects` leaves open once the root is named. Proposes saying the retired folder is ignored and naming the folder in use, the wording the owner's, and a CLAUDE.md rule that a message telling the user to move or rename something checks the destination first. Every run now says the folder is ignored and names the one in use, as proposed (CLI-931, owner 2026-10-24); the CLAUDE.md rule is not written. Partial.
- `2026-10-23-a-null-cost-that-also-meant-not-read-yet-let-the-switch-question-claim-the-costliest-answer` — the editor's stack-switch question took its sentence from `stackSwitchCost`, which answered `string | null`, and its `null` meant both "this stack replaces what is on screen" and "an account's payload is not read yet". The dialog rendered `?? discarding(skillCount)`, so before the payload arrived it said "Switching replaces your current setup, discarding 1 selected skill…" of a stack built on another marketplace, which discards nothing, and the payload was read with `getState()` during render, so its arrival never corrected the sentence. Fixed: a `StackSwitchCost` union (unread, replaces, elsewhere) mapped by an exhaustive switch, with the account payloads subscribed. `use-apply-stack-request.test.ts` (4 red first) and the two held-payload specs in `saved-stack-apply.spec.ts` (red with `getState()` put back) hold it. `editor-and-worker.md` › "…never "A was attempted"" already names one `null` answering for two readings, framed around failed work; proposes widening it to an answer still loading. What Switch does while loading, and after a failed read, were open owner questions. Resolved.
- `2026-10-23-a-failed-account-payload-read-leaves-no-trace-so-the-switch-questions-loading-state-cannot-end` — `readUnheldPayloads` in the editor's `account-store.ts` keeps only the payload reads that succeeded, so a failed read leaves the store as an unanswered one does, and the stack-switch question's `unread` sentence, "This stack is built on loading…", stays until the dialog closes. `readPayloads`' only production caller is `stack-grid.tsx`'s effect on `accountStacks`, so nothing reads again while it is open. Switch fetches again, and a second failure closes the dialog with nothing applied and nothing on screen. Fixed from the owner's answers to `q-e2c-switch-while-loading` ("Switch waits until the stack has loaded") and `q-e2c-load-fails` ("Say it failed"): `failedReads` in the store records a failed read, `stackSwitchCost` answers `failed`, and the dialog holds Switch disabled while "loading…" shows, says "Couldn't load this stack." with Retry in Switch's place once a read fails, and applies the payload it read rather than fetching again. Specs in `account-store.test.ts`, `use-apply-stack-request.test.ts` and `saved-stack-apply.spec.ts` were red first. Proposes an "A arrived" corollary for `editor-and-worker.md`: a store reading for a surface holds a failed read as its own state. Resolved.
- `2026-10-23-a-button-disabled-by-its-own-press-drops-keyboard-focus-to-the-page` — a natively disabled `<button>` cannot hold focus, so where a button's own press sets the state that disables it, a keyboard press drops focus to `body`. The stack-switch question's Retry did, becoming a held Switch while "loading…" showed and leaving the dialog. `buttonVariants` gains an `aria-disabled:` pair, the question renders one action with `focusableWhenDisabled`, and the Marketplace dialog's Load takes it too; specs in `button.stories.tsx`, `saved-stack-apply.spec.ts` and `dialog-focus.spec.ts` were red first. Composer's Send, the add-skill dialog's Add, the save dialog's Save and the roster's Share remain. Proposes a `packages/ui/CLAUDE.md` rule: a `Button` disabled by its own press takes `focusableWhenDisabled`. Partial.
- `2026-10-23-a-mutant-served-through-the-editors-vite-config-rewrites-the-shared-dependency-cache-unless-it-names-its-own` — the out-of-tree Vite config of `2026-10-21-a-mutant-only-playwright-reaches-can-be-served-to-the-dev-server-without-writing-the-shared-tree` leaves the source untouched but hashes differently from the editor's own, so Vite re-optimises into the editor's shared dependency cache (`Re-optimizing dependencies because vite config has changed`), which `git diff` cannot see and a concurrent `test:e2e` dev server reads. A `cacheDir` outside the tree stopped it on the four CLI-949 runs after. Proposes adding that to the 2026-10-21 method in `docs/architecture.md` › "Mutation testing". Discovery only. Open.
- `2026-10-23-the-summary-panels-content-specs-mounted-it-live-while-every-command-prints-it` — `summary-panel.test.tsx`'s header and summary specs mounted `<SummaryPanel>` live through ink-testing-library while every command prints it through `summaryPanelText` (`renderToString`, which skips effects), so they pinned a frame no user sees. Moved onto the printed path; proposes a § 6 rule that a printed-only component is specced through the function that prints it.
- `2026-10-23-a-pattern-a-spec-escapes-by-hand-escapes-only-the-characters-its-author-saw` — doctor's row patterns were escaped spec by spec (`.replace(/[()]/g, ...)`, which handles only parentheses), and one spec interpolated the marketplace address unescaped, so every `.` matched any character. Now built once by `doctorRow` in `e2e/pages/constants.ts`, which escapes every part, and the two unit specs that took the skip glyph from the product mirror it instead. Every other spec now escapes the paths, URLs, ids and mirrored text it puts into a pattern with one tested helper, `matchedAsWritten` in `src/cli/lib/__tests__/helpers/`, which `doctorRow` uses too. Resolved.
- `2026-10-24-an-e2e-panel-block-can-be-pinned-only-as-literal-lines-since-constants-mirror-its-cells-not-its-lines` — the e2e vocabulary for the summary panel (`panelRow`, `STEP_TEXT.PANEL_*`) pins one cell, and every panel line holds two columns, so a spec held to "end a block on the line after it" can only write the lines literally; `init-from-prints-its-panel-whole` and `list-keeps-long-names-whole` already do, as inline snapshots, and no standard says so. At a PTY the path line under `uninstall`'s panel wraps by the temp directory's length, so no block may end on it.
- `2026-10-24-the-persist-write-standard-says-assert-identity-not-storage-while-the-guard-specs-beside-it-watch-the-slot` — `editor-and-worker.md` says a "writes nothing" spec asserts state identity, not storage, because node has no `localStorage` to watch; `storeOnAWatchedSlot` in `config-store.test.ts` stubs one, and the `setAgentOption` and catalogue-refusal guard specs prove "no write" by the slot alone. Identity is the stronger claim (`set` never called), and a storage-layer skip would pass the slot spec. The new `setSkillOption` specs assert both. Proposed deleting "not storage" and its reason from the standard. The standard now says to assert both, with two exceptions (no slot; a guard at the storage door), and to prove each spec against its own guard. The guard specs in `config-store.test.ts` and `saved-stack-store.test.ts` check both, and each failed with its guard removed in an out-of-tree config. One had gone vacuous: "writes nothing for an option set on a skill it cannot place" pressed the scope a fresh entry already has, so `setSkillOption`'s new in-effect check answered it before the catalogue guard did. Resolved.
- `2026-10-24-reading-a-marketplace-folders-catalogue-seats-it-so-every-later-display-name-reads-that-catalogue-alone` — `loadMarketplaceMatrix` reads like a pure read but seats the catalogue it built, which holds only that marketplace, so every later `getSkillDisplayName` names a public-catalogue skill by its id. `uninstall` reads a local marketplace folder to name its plugin rows and puts the caller's catalogue back in a `finally`, which `uninstall.test.ts` now pins (`names the plugin by the folder and a kept skill only the catalogue it started with holds by that catalogue, in one panel`). Resolved by CLI-957: the loader no longer seats what it reads, and hands the stack filter the catalogue it built.
- `2026-10-24-a-press-on-the-skill-option-already-in-effect-saved-again-and-the-persist-write-census-had-not-recorded-it` — `setSkillOption` wrote and tracked a press already in effect, a census hit of "Under `persist`, `set` IS the write" that its survivor table never recorded. Now guarded through a generic `alreadyInEffect`, judged as pressed so plugin on an eject-only skill still writes and reports eject; proposes that row for the table, and reading `applyStack` on its own stack. The row is in the table. `applyStack` and `applySavedStack` (the same miss) now return before `set`, and `applyStack` before its event, when the selection after `keepingHeld` equals the one in effect, compared by value. Resolved.
- `2026-10-24-createTestSource-drops-a-skills-displayName-and-names-every-skill-by-its-id` — `TestSkill` declares a `displayName` that `createTestSkill` always fills, but `createTestSource` (source and local skills) and `writeSourceSkill` write the skill's id as its name instead, so a spec needing a folder that names a skill by something other than its id has to write the metadata.yaml again (the put-back spec in `uninstall.test.ts` does). Proposes honouring the field, after measuring which specs pin a source skill's name as its id, or dropping it from the type. Resolved: the writers write the name, and the specs that pinned the id now expect it.
- `2026-10-24-the-grids-at-once-door-read-an-account-stack-through-a-fetch-the-question-never-used-so-its-failure-went-unsaid` — applying an account's stack has two doors in `use-apply-stack-request.ts`; the question read through the account store and worded a failure, the grid's at-once door read with a bare fetch and dropped the failure arm, so a failed load said nothing and a loaded one never lit its cell. Both now share one read through the store; proposes that "An act reachable through two controls is a module" say the doors share the read too.
- `2026-10-24-cli-run-strips-one-final-newline-from-each-stream-so-a-block-ending-a-stream-has-no-line-after-it` — `CLI.run` and `runCLI` capture through `execa` with its default `stripFinalNewline`, so each stream loses its last newline and CLAUDE.md's "end on the line after the block" cannot match a block that ends a stream; `output` and `combined` also glue stdout's last line onto stderr's first. Met by the CLI-931 rework's stderr notice, pinned with `toBe` on the whole stream. No spec is wrong today. Proposes a sentence in that rule: pin a block that ends a stream with `toBe`, never by dropping the trailing `""`. Open.
- `2026-10-24-a-load-result-carries-an-unreachable-flag-that-no-production-code-reads` — `FetchResult.unreachable` is copied into `SourceLoadResult.unreachable` and only unit specs read it, so CLI-958 had to pick its value for a copy kept over a refused version check with nothing to say which was right. Proposes deleting the field and widening CLAUDE.md's no-reader rule from display types to any result type. Open.
- `2026-10-24-a-list-read-answered-every-failure-as-an-empty-account-so-each-failed-load-carried-every-save-in-again` — `listStacks` answered `[]` for a 500, a network error and an unreadable 200, and `owedOldestFirst` reads an empty list as a first sign-in, so every signed-in load whose read failed uploaded every browser save again (and, with the writes failing too, marked them all owed for a false rename prompt). Seen twice before as a lead and left. Now a result value; `refreshOnce` adopts only on a read that arrived and records a failed one as `listRefusal`. Proposes extending "the condition that hides B is 'A arrived'" from what a surface draws to what a read decides to write. The 401-after-session case is left for an owner ruling. Resolved.
- `2026-10-24-the-old-folder-line-named-claudes-folder-by-a-literal-so-every-codex-root-was-told-agents-inc-reads-claudes` — `retiredSourceFolderIgnored` built the folder agents-inc reads with `sourceDirName("claude")`, so a root holding a Codex installation was told agents-inc reads `.agents-inc/claude/`. The private `PROVIDER_OF_A_NEW_INSTALLATION` cannot stop a literal, which is the same silent default. Now the init hook passes `providerInUse(root)` per root. Proposes that a folder builder never take its provider from a literal outside tests and `consts`, with the census grep as the check. Resolved.
- `2026-10-24-a-provider-a-dist-run-names-outlives-it-so-an-in-process-spec-of-the-init-hook-passes-against-the-defect` — oclif loads the `init` hook from `dist/`, so a hook spec runs through `runCliCommand`, where a `--provider` an earlier run (or a first attempt) named lasts the worker: the CLI-967 row passed against its defect with its twin first, and after the fix a bare `list` following `list --provider codex` still names codex over a Claude root. A member of the 2026-10-22 dist-reset finding that its source-import option cannot cover. Proposes the hook reset the run's provider first, or `runCliCommand` reset dist's. Resolved by the first: the hook resets before anything else.
- `2026-10-24-a-folder-whose-config-declares-nothing-is-an-installation-to-the-roster-and-not-to-the-listing` — `detectInstallations` counts any folder holding a `config.ts`, while `detectInstallationInDir` also skips one that declares nothing. So from a project whose config declares nothing, under a HOME holding both installations, the roster `installationsInPlay` builds judges the project, `list` shows HOME's Claude installation, and no line names the other (hand-run on the built CLI). The acting commands' refusal reads that roster too, so they may act on one of two without refusing (inference). Proposes one definition of an installation in a scope for both reads, in `packages/cli/CLAUDE.md` › Scope Awareness. Partial: the roster, the listing and the fan-out now skip such a config (CLI-971); the standard is not written.
- `2026-10-24-the-edit-from-plugin-specs-applied-a-configuration-and-checked-only-what-it-printed` — eleven specs in `edit-from-plugins-says-what-it-did.test.ts` applied a configuration and checked only its output, against CLAUDE.md's "verify config AND filesystem". None could answer n: each pins a line printed after the confirm. So each now also checks the config's skills, the local copies, the global config where one sits beside, and the mock host's exact calls, and each check reddens under a mutant of its subject. The checks found a plugin installed beside its global copy requested twice (switch, then scope move), pinned as a `KNOWN GAP` spec. Proposes stating in CLAUDE.md that a mocked host's calls are the plugin side's state. Partial: all four spec files are repaired (CLI-976 did the three siblings) and the double install is fixed (CLI-975); the CLAUDE.md sentences are not written.
- `2026-10-25-the-editor-suites-answer-a-stack-save-themselves-so-the-workers-name-refusal-never-reaches-them` — the api-mocks README makes that package the one description of the worker, yet the editor's suites answer `POST /stacks` with five local handlers in three files (`stacks.test.ts`, `account-store.test.ts`, `e2e/support/auth.ts`), each a 201 to any name, because the shared mock has no stateful account. Once the worker refuses a name the account already holds (owner, 2026-10-24), none of them give that refusal. One editor spec saves `SAVED_STACK.name` against `signedInHandlers`, whose list holds it. Proposes that a local handler delegate the worker's refusals or say which it leaves out, with the census in the README. Discovery only. Open.
- `2026-10-25-better-auths-oauth-callback-leaves-an-unhandled-rejection-in-the-workers-pool-so-no-sign-in-spec-can-finish-green` — every request to Better Auth's `/api/auth/callback/github` ends in a thrown 302 `APIError` that the `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers` pool also reports as an unhandled rejection, so Vitest exits 1 with every test passing. Reproduced through Better Auth's own handler with no Hono. So no spec pins the cookie a GitHub sign-in sets. Proposes a narrow `onUnhandledError` filter in `apps/server/vitest.config.ts` for 3xx `APIError`s only, with its reason in `editor-and-worker.md`. Discovery only. Open.
- `2026-10-25-the-display-name-rule-says-where-a-name-may-go-and-never-where-one-must-so-the-notices-under-a-from-panel-printed-ids` — CLAUDE.md's display-name rule lists where a name may go and how a row finds one, never where one is required. So the five notices printed with an `init --from` / `edit --from` panel printed ids beside the panel's names until the owner ruled "Name them as the box does" (2026-10-24). A census of `utils/messages.ts` finds six more id-printing lines the ruling does not cover. Proposes one CLAUDE.md clause: a notice printed with a `--from` panel names each skill as the panel row for it does. Discovery only; the CLI-972 reds pin the ruling. Open.
- `2026-10-26-a-mutate-range-of-one-bare-line-matches-no-file-and-stryker-exits-0-having-mutated-nothing` — SERVER-08 told the runner to pass `--mutate apps/server/src/crawl.ts:<line>`. Stryker reads a bare `file:58` as a glob, matches nothing, dry-runs and exits 0 with an all-zero table; `file:58-58` instruments the 6 mutants. `docs/architecture.md` warns about an empty `--mutate` but never gives the range form. Proposes one sentence there: write `file:L-L`, and read the `Instrumented N source file(s)` line before trusting the table.
- `2026-10-26-the-switch-dialog-worded-a-catalogue-refusal-onto-the-seams-sentence-and-dropped-its-kind-so-every-cause-was-offered-a-token` — `fetchCatalog` returns a code (`kind`) and a sentence with no full stop. The Marketplace dialog shows the sentence alone and offers a token only for `unauthorized`. The switch dialog drops `kind` and appends its own sentence, so the two run together and a token is offered for an unreachable GitHub, a 5xx and an unparsable catalogue. `editor-and-worker.md`'s "surface owns the words" table omits the catalogue seam. Proposes adding it, and saying a surface extending a seam's sentence reads its code and closes the sentence first. Partial: the switch dialog now keeps `kind`, closes the sentence and offers a token only for `unauthorized`; the standard's row is pending.
- `2026-10-26-two-saves-sent-together-to-the-worker-in-its-test-pool-never-race-so-the-race-spec-passes-without-the-guard` — the race spec SERVER-10 added (two `POST /stacks` through `Promise.all` over `SELF`, expecting `[201, 409]`) passes with the worker's in-statement guard replaced by `where 1`, because requests in the Workers test pool never interleave between one handler's D1 read and its write. A probe that lands a same-name write through `env.DATABASE.prepare` just before the guarded statement shows the guard is load-bearing: the real code answers 409, while the mutant answers 201 and 200 and holds the name twice. Proposes an `editor-and-worker.md` rule that a race spec must land one request inside the other and assert it did, with a Stryker run on the file as the check. The forced interleave has since landed as a spec, and a correction records the `Promise.all` spec catching the mutant in 4 of 10 runs, by chance; the rule is unwritten. Partial.
- `2026-10-26-the-e2e-config-writers-round-trip-guard-rewrote-a-project-config-with-its-inlined-global-still-inside-so-every-global-config-write-was-refused` — `refuseUnreachableConfig` (e2e `test-utils.ts`) read back a project config written with `globalConfig` and wrote it again whole, so the inlined global entries came out twice and every non-empty `globalConfig` write was refused. It now splits off the inlined global with `splitConfigByScope`, as the product does before writing, and `fixture-configs-round-trip` pins this (mutation-checked). The `globalImportPath` (spread) form cannot be honoured, since a field the project states and one it inherits read back the same, and no product path writes it, so `writeProjectConfig` no longer offers it. Proposes an `e2e/anti-patterns.md` rule: a read-back guard undoes what its writer added, or the helper drops the option. Partial: the rule is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-a-refusal-of-a-links-own-shape-decided-over-what-loaded-lets-a-failed-load-install-the-link-it-refuses` — a link pairing a project-scoped skill with a sub-agent resting at global is refused by `init --from` and `edit --from`, but when that skill's metadata.yaml will not load the same link installs the sub-agent globally with only the skip warning: `seedToInstallSelection` judges pairs over loaded skills only, a premise the change that installs sub-agents past a failed skill (CLI-915) expired. Proposes a `CLAUDE.md` clause: a refusal of the link's own shape is decided over the link, and a `--from` refusal's control includes a failed load. Discovery only; the CLI-935 reds are in `seed-to-config.test.ts` and `from-refuses-an-unwritable-pair-whether-its-skill-loads.e2e.test.ts`. Partial: the decode now judges every pair the payload names; the clause is pending.
- `2026-10-26-a-tree-snapshot-of-files-reads-a-refused-run-that-left-empty-folders-behind-as-one-that-wrote-nothing` — `readTreeSnapshot` (e2e `test-utils.ts`) keeps files only, so a refused run that died inside a `mkdir` and left empty folders snapshots the same as one that wrote nothing. Measured on CLI-936: unguarded, `init --from` an id over 255 bytes left an empty `.claude/skills/` in the project. The new refusal specs pair the snapshot with the folder's absence; the helper is unchanged. Open.
- `2026-10-26-an-edit-fixture-that-replaced-an-ejected-skill-md-whole-stopped-it-loading-as-local-so-the-force-spec-tested-another-skill` — writing the CLI-933 reds, an edit that replaced an ejected `SKILL.md` whole dropped its frontmatter. The skill stopped loading as local, so `eject skills --force` copied that one skill, the edit came back, and the spec's subject passed against the defect. A green unit spec also reached `No skills found in the marketplace to eject.` through `--force`, which pinned the defect. The edits now append, the green pin moved to a state reachable without `--force`, and the specs check restored content because the copier records a local skill at its destination in place. Proposes that an edit fixture leave the artefact valid for its reader, in `e2e/test-data.md`. Open.
- `2026-10-26-a-load-that-took-a-link-off-the-address-had-its-notice-cleared-by-the-opening-that-address-change-triggered` — a Marketplace load, a switch or a saved stack leaving a shared link opened this browser's own configuration and named what it could not place, then took `fromId` off the address; that re-ran `useCatalogFirst`'s effect, keyed on `fromId`, which cleared the notice and reopened a slot already open, so the ids were lost. Fixed (EDITOR-114): leaving moved into `useLeaveTheLink`, whose one-shot marker the effect takes on every opening, skipping only the bare address's opening. Proposes an `editor-and-worker.md` rule that a handler which does an address's work and then changes the address tells the effect keyed on it. Partial.
- `2026-10-26-the-workers-race-guard-compared-the-names-a-read-handed-back-so-one-the-store-could-not-hand-back-whole-made-every-later-save-fail` — the worker's stack name accepted half of a surrogate pair, which D1 stores as its bytes and hands back as three U+FFFD, and the race guard compared the account's stored names against the text its read handed back, so that row never matched: every later save and rename of the account lost five attempts and answered 500. Fixed by refusing such a name with a 400 and comparing `hex(name)` in the guard; specs hold each half. Proposes an `editor-and-worker.md` rule to compare stored bytes in SQL conditions and refuse ill-formed UTF-16 at the schema. Resolved.
- `2026-10-26-five-from-specs-each-declare-their-own-install-surface-snapshot-and-one-reads-a-fourth-folder` — five `--from` specs each declare `snapshotInstallSurfaces` for their "writes nothing" claim, and one of them also reads the Codex home, so the same claim covers different folders spec by spec. CLI-987 added the fifth copy because `e2e/helpers/` was outside its lane. Proposes one shared helper and a CLAUDE.md clause saying a helper copied into a second spec moves to `e2e/helpers/`. Open.
- `2026-10-26-a-rewrite-read-off-a-tarballs-modification-time-is-invisible-within-the-second-on-a-filesystem-keeping-whole-seconds` — `gigetDownload` decided whether giget wrote the tarball it extracted by comparing the tarball's modification time before and after. On a filesystem keeping whole seconds, a rewrite within the second left the time unchanged, so doctor refused a fresh download as giget's saved copy whenever the host refused HEAD. Fixed (CLI-938): each saved tarball's giget record is marked with a leading newline, which JSON ignores and giget never writes, and is put back by rename unless giget rewrote it; one run at a time holds a lock on giget's provider folder from mark to put-back, since the marks cover every marketplace under it, and a lock whose run ended is taken over. Byte comparison, ctime, and restoring a stamped time with `utimes` (inexact for 14 of 20 files sampled) are recorded as non-fixes. It lists the tooling sites that still read a rewrite from modification time. Resolved.
- `2026-10-26-a-no-request-check-over-a-spawned-wrangler-read-the-developers-own-skills-cache-so-a-leaked-agent-variable-passed-wherever-that-cache-was-fresh` — the server dev spec's "asks GitHub for nothing" passed with an AI agent's variable let through wherever the developer's wrangler skills cache was under a day old, because `devEnvironment` handed wrangler the caller's `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`; it failed only once that cache had aged. The helper now gives wrangler a config folder in the run's scratch, pinned by a `devEnvironment` row and a let-through control, each watched red. Proposes the rule for `standards/editor-and-worker.md`: a spawned third-party CLI gets config and cache folders of the run's own, with a control in the same file.
- `2026-10-26-a-spec-reading-a-file-outside-the-cli-package-is-not-hashed-into-its-test-task-so-a-change-to-that-file-alone-replays-a-cached-pass` — `packages/cli/turbo.json` hashes the CLI's `test` task over the package plus `todo/` and `CLAUDE.md`, and its own comment says a tree a checker reads outside the package belongs there. Three specs read outside it unlisted: the push-hook spec (`.husky/pre-push`), the CI-workflow check in `generate-compile-package.test.ts` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) and the repository-wide symbol scan (`apps/`). A change to one of those alone replays a cached green locally, including from the push hook, which calls turbo without `--force`; CI runs fresh. The task now hashes every outside file the census found except the symbol scan's, and `scripts/test-task-hashes-the-files-its-specs-read.test.ts` pins the list; the docs rule and whether to hash the scan are open.
- `2026-10-26-a-module-that-tested-its-own-folder-by-substring-read-any-checkout-under-a-dist-named-folder-as-the-build` — `consts.ts` asked whether it ran from the build with `__dirname.includes("/dist")`, so a source checkout under any folder named `dist…` set `PROJECT_ROOT` to `src/` and code reading the CLI's own files there failed. Fixed (CLI-930): the folder the module sits in decides it (`path.basename(__dirname) === "dist"`), with specs for source and build layouts under dist-named parents. A census found no other live instance. Proposes a `clean-code-standards.md` rule: test a file's location by path segment, never by substring of an absolute path. Resolved.
- `2026-10-26-carried-names-were-checked-for-shape-and-never-for-the-file-systems-length-limit-so-an-overlong-one-failed-in-mkdir` — a carried skill's id and its files' names were checked for separators, `..` and control characters but never for the 255-byte name limit, so `init --from` and `edit --from` died on a raw ENAMETOOLONG, `init` after asking and leaving folders behind. Fixed for the id (CLI-936): `registerExternalSkills` refuses an id over 255 UTF-8 bytes by name before anything is seated. A carried file name over the limit still fails mid-write, and minting still accepts both. Proposes a `CLAUDE.md` Data Integrity clause: a payload string that becomes a path segment is refused for shape and length, at install and at mint. Partial.
- `2026-10-26-turbos-full-screen-view-inside-script-printed-nothing-and-never-exited-so-a-120-column-gate-held-the-shared-lock` — `turbo.json` sets `"ui": "tui"`, so a 120-column gate run as `script -qec "stty cols 120 rows 40; npx turbo run …"` from a background shell opened turbo's full-screen view, wrote 0 bytes in 25 minutes, ignored SIGTERM and held the shared CLI lock; the same command with `--ui=stream` finished in 1m19s. Proposes that every gate running turbo inside a pseudo-terminal passes `--ui=stream`. Open.
- `2026-10-26-cors-mounted-on-a-path-and-on-its-wildcard-ran-twice-on-the-bare-path-because-honos-wildcard-matches-it-too` — the worker mounted its one CORS middleware on `/configs` and `/configs/*`, `/skills` and `/skills/*`, and `/stacks` and `/stacks/*`. Hono's `/x/*` also matches `/x`, so both mounts ran on the bare path and served routes answered `vary: Origin, Origin`. Fixed (SERVER-07): one mount per path, `/x/*` where sub-paths are served and `/x` where none are, held by the Vary table in `apps/server/src/index.test.ts` and the `/skills` spec. A census found no other doubled mount. Proposes one sentence in `editor-and-worker.md`. Partial: the sentence is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-a-member-of-an-exported-constant-table-that-nothing-names-passes-every-gate` — four `METADATA_KEYS` members have no reader (CLI-942), and knip, `tsc` and lint cannot see an unread member of an exported object. A find-references census found unread members in ten more tables in `consts.ts`, `exit-codes.ts` and `messages.ts`, among them `EXIT_CODES.NETWORK_ERROR`, which the www reference documents and no command raises. CLI-821 covers five of the `UI_SYMBOLS` members. Proposes a § 9 rule in `clean-code-standards.md`, plus a find-references checker if the owner wants one. Partial: CLI-942 deleted the four members; the rule and the sibling tables are open.
- `2026-10-26-the-switch-dialog-filled-loadcosts-null-with-loses-nothing-though-a-recovery-is-null-because-it-replaces-the-screen` — `loadCost` returns `null` for three reasons. The switch dialog filled every one with "loses nothing", which is false when the switch finishes a parked link or stack, because that replaces the screen. The dialog now words that case itself. The standards sentence (word a `null` only once you know why it is `null`) is pending.
- `2026-10-26-a-turbo-gate-without-continue-cancels-its-other-tasks-when-one-fails-so-a-cancelled-lint-read-as-a-pass` — CLI-938's gate ran `turbo run lint typecheck test` without `--continue`. turbo's default (`never`) cancels the other tasks once one fails, so when other lanes' specs failed in `test`, `lint` stopped before printing a result. Its blank section read as a pass, and the lane's 2 lint errors reached review. The summary names only the failed task. Proposes that `briefing.md`'s Gates row pass `--continue` wherever one turbo run carries several tasks, and that reports read each task's result from the summary's counts. Open.
- `2026-10-26-the-two-skill-loaders-disagree-on-which-failed-loads-are-warned-so-a-local-skill-with-no-skill-md-is-still-skipped-silently` — `packages/cli/CLAUDE.md` says `warn` is for user issues and `verbose` for diagnostics, but not which a skipped skill is. The marketplace loader said only a missing SKILL.md under `--verbose`, pinned by a "warns about nothing" spec that became a red (CLI-934). The local loader still says two skips of a folder its own metadata describes only under `--verbose`: no SKILL.md, and an unreadable frontmatter. Proposes one Logging sentence in `packages/cli/CLAUDE.md`, then a row for the local two. Partial: the marketplace loader now warns it (CLI-934); the local two are open.
- `2026-10-26-a-marketplace-skill-read-through-the-merged-matrix-is-the-local-copy-so-eject-force-copied-nothing` — after the local merge, an earlier eject's copies take the marketplace's entries' place. `eject skills --force` dropped them as local and resolved the rest through `getSkillById`, so it copied nothing. Fixed (CLI-933): `SourceLoadResult.marketplaceSkills` keeps the entries from before the merge, and eject filters and copies from them. Proposes a `CLAUDE.md` rule that a marketplace's own skill is read from that snapshot, never from the merged matrix. Partial: the rule is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-a-census-of-sanitiser-call-sites-cannot-see-a-validator-message-that-calls-none` — the 2026-10-22 line-break finding found its siblings by grepping `stripTerminalControls(`, so a validator message that sanitises nothing was invisible to it. `checkSnakeCaseKeys` and `checkDirNameMatchesSkillId` in `source-validator.ts` quote a marketplace's key and `SKILL.md` name raw, escape and line feed alike, and `doctor` prints them as lines of their own (reproduced through `validateSource`); `plugin-validator.ts`'s two `name must be kebab-case` lines read the same way, not run. CLI-988 covers `formatZodIssue` only. Proposes the print-side grep, and that 5.8's split names a quoted key, id or name as an in-line value. Open.
- `2026-10-26-the-rule-deciding-which-stack-names-collide-was-written-three-times-and-only-the-workers-copy-was-checked-beyond-ascii` — which stack names an account cannot both hold (trimmed, in any case) was one line declared as `sameName` in the editor's `save-name.ts`, the worker's `stacks.ts` and the api-mocks `handlers.ts`, each pinned only by its own specs, and only the worker's specs tried a non-ASCII name, the case SQLite's ASCII-only `lower()` gets wrong. Fixed (REPO-47): `sameStackName` in `packages/matrix/src/stack-name.ts` is the one definition, and the e2e stub keeps its deliberate mirror. Proposes an `editor-and-worker.md` rule (a decision the editor anticipates and the worker enforces has one definition in `packages/matrix`). No honest gate exists, because a text scan misses exactly the copies that would be wrong.
- `2026-10-26-a-toast-is-a-dialog-to-a-role-selector-so-a-count-of-open-dialogs-waited-out-a-five-second-toast` — Base UI gives each toast `role="dialog"` and `aria-modal="false"`. `accounts.spec.ts` counted open dialogs by bare role, so 'waits behind a stack switch's confirm' counted the sub-agent toast its own React toggle raised. It passed only when the 5-second toast left inside the assertion's 5-second retry (1 of 6 failed unloaded, 1 of 3 at load 46). Fixed (EDITOR-99): the count leaves out `[aria-modal="false"]`. Proposes a line in `apps/editor/e2e/README.md`. Partial: the README line is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-the-editor-e2e-dev-server-pushes-a-file-another-lane-saves-into-open-pages-so-a-spec-loses-its-selection-mid-test` — the editor suite serves through Vite's dev server with HMR on, so a file another lane saves mid-run re-runs a store module in the open page and empties the selection. This was EDITOR-99's 'holds the dialog while the save is in flight' and 'an offline mint keeps the name, and Save again stores it once online' flakes: roster Save stayed disabled for 30 s. Saving `config-store.ts` every 3 s, bytes unchanged, made 3 of 10 runs fail; a no-HMR copy of the config passed 10 of 10. Load alone gave 0 failures in 12 runs each. Proposes `vite.e2e.config.ts` with `server.hmr: false` as the webServer's config, and a README line. Open.
- `2026-10-26-a-spec-that-boots-the-editor-only-to-own-its-origin-spends-a-page-load-of-its-budget-and-under-load-the-budget-is-page-loads` — seeding `localStorage` needs the origin, and `accounts.spec.ts` booted the whole editor to get it. Under load page loads are 75–96% of a multi-load test's time, and three tests that load four or five times hit the 30 s timeout at median load 55. Fixed in that file (EDITOR-99): `atTheOrigin` seeds at a stubbed empty document. Proposes a README line. Partial: 19 seed call sites in 8 other spec files are unchecked.
- `2026-10-26-a-behaviour-the-www-guides-stated-on-four-pages-was-corrected-on-the-three-sharing-its-wording-and-stayed-false-on-the-fourth` — the guides described what a later command does with a saved marketplace copy on four pages. CLI-939 qualified the three its row named, which shared "Every load revalidates its cached copy"; `use-a-private-marketplace.md` said "falls back to the cached copy and warns" and stayed false for a copy saved without an ETag until review found it by the behaviour's nouns. That page now links to `common-problems#a-marketplace-that-will-not-load`. Proposes a `documentation-bible.md` rule for `apps/www`: a behaviour is described in one section and other pages link to it; a sweep searches the behaviour's vocabulary, not the false sentence. Partial: the rule is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-an-executor-re-derived-its-share-of-a-plan-from-the-config-diff-so-a-skill-that-switched-mode-and-scope-reached-two-executors` — `detectMigrations` partitions changed skills into `toEject`, `toPlugin` and `scopeChanges`, but `edit --from` never read `scopeChanges`: `applyScopeChanges` rebuilt its set from the config diff, so a skill switching mode and scope was installed by the switch and asked for again by the scope move (and, project copy to global plugin, a project plugin that never existed was uninstalled). Proposes for `clean-code-standards.md`: where a planner partitions items among executors, each executor takes its share of that plan. Partial: CLI-975 fixed it and CLI-981's exact host-call specs redden under a mutant restoring it; the rule is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-createTestEnvironment-puts-the-project-inside-the-fake-home-and-the-helper-table-does-not-say-which-layout-each-helper-gives` — `createTestEnvironment()` makes `<fakeHome>/project`, while `ProjectBuilder.dualScope()` and the unit suite's `setupIsolatedHome` make the project and HOME siblings. `e2e/test-data.md` describes it as "Creates tempDir/fakeHome/project" and its helper-choice paragraph names no layout, so a spec (126 spec files plus the seed wrappers) gets one or the other by its helper. No product behaviour depends on it today. Proposes naming each setup's layout in `test-data.md`. Open.
- `2026-10-26-a-census-of-a-copied-writer-by-its-name-found-two-copies-and-a-third-under-another-name-wrote-the-same-folder` — the writer of an old `.claude-src/` folder was copied into three e2e spec files; two reports counted the two named `oldFolderIn` and missed `retiredFolderIn`. Now `writeRetiredSourceFolder` in `e2e/helpers/test-utils.ts`, and a census by what the writer emits finds no spec-file copy. Proposes that `e2e/anti-patterns.md` count a writer's copies by what it writes. Partial: the sentence is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-the-findings-check-asked-the-checkouts-git-what-is-ignored-so-an-export-read-ignored-paths-as-missing` — `check-findings-frontmatter.ts` asked the checkout's `.git` whether a path is ignored, so a `git archive` export (no `.git`) counted two git-ignored references as missing while CI and checkouts passed; a git failure answered "not ignored" silently. CLI-964 asks a throwaway bare git directory that reads only the repository's `.gitignore` files, and throws on a git failure; four fixture rows outside any repository hold it. Proposes for `clean-code-standards.md`: a gate reads a fact the repository states from the tracked file, never from the machine, and a failed read is an error. Partial: the rule is unwritten.
- `2026-10-26-a-host-mock-does-not-reach-a-command-run-through-dist-so-uninstall-specs-ask-the-developers-real-claude` — `runCliCommand` loads `dist/` by native import, so a `vi.mock` of the host never reaches its runs and the host spawns whichever `claude` PATH finds. `init-from-says-what-it-did` had no mock, and its three held-skill specs spawned `claude` under two skip mutants (one stayed green). A census of every dist-run spec file found `uninstall.test.ts` asking the real `claude` today (18 calls), with four specs green only because it answers "not installed". CLI-983 put `useClaudeStub` in the first file. CLI-989 put it in `uninstall.test.ts`, whose six plugin specs now assert the uninstall at both scopes in either order, and made the stand-in refuse a plugin uninstall the way Claude does for a plugin it does not hold. Proposes a CLAUDE.md sentence and a refusing `claude` on PATH for the unit projects. Partial: the sentence and the refusing `claude` remain.
- `2026-10-26-two-reads-of-compiled-sub-agents-count-only-claudes-format-whatever-the-installation-is-on` — `list`'s `Agents:` count globbed `agentCodec("claude")` and `doctor`'s Agents content row lists `listAgentMdFiles`, so a Codex installation's `.toml` role files went uncounted; hidden in a home holding both while both read the Claude global. `list` now takes folder and format from one provider read (hand-run, unpinned); the doctor row is unrepaired. Partial.
- `2026-10-26-a-reference-census-of-a-deleted-field-misses-a-spec-that-expects-it-in-an-untyped-object-literal` — deleting `Installation.skillsDir` (CLI-985), a language-service reference census and `tsc` on all three projects both missed `detect-project.test.ts`, whose `detectedAt` helper returns an untyped literal compared with `toStrictEqual`; only the full unit run found it. The spec is fixed. Proposes one CLAUDE.md sentence: find the specs asserting a deleted member with a full unit run, or a grep for the member with its siblings' keys. Partial.
- `2026-10-26-a-multi-agent-run-spends-most-of-its-tokens-re-reading-each-agents-own-context` — over 24 hours of session f0e04877, 203 sub-agents used 4.9B tokens, 94% cache reads and about 1% output; cost is steps x context per step (median agent about 17M, orchestrator about 2.7B alone). Orchestrator compaction lowered to 200k; eight levers (a-h) listed to try later, lever e conflicts with `briefing.md`. Open: none adopted but h.
- `2026-10-29-uninstall-at-home-read-the-project-scope-folders-through-a-defaulted-scope-so-a-codex-global-installs-copies-stayed` — `detectUninstallTarget` called `resolveInstallPaths(projectDir)` with no scope, so at home it read the project folders; on Codex a global eject install's copies in `$CODEX_HOME/skills` survived `uninstall --yes`. Fixed by passing the target's scope; pending: make `scope` required in `install-base-dir.ts` (another lane's). Partial.
- `2026-10-30-the-release-checklist-commits-bun-lock-if-bun-install-moved-it-and-never-says-to-run-it` — `bun.lock`'s `packages/cli` entry read `0.170.2` against a `0.175.0` package: the checklist commits the lock "if `bun install` moved it" and never runs `bun install`, so the next lane to install anything inherits the release line. Open.
- `2026-10-30-the-editor-e2e-readme-says-every-locator-lives-in-pages-and-twenty-seven-specs-hold-their-own` — the editor e2e README says all locators live in `pages/`, never in a spec; a grep finds locator calls on 110 lines across 27 spec files (2026-10-30) and nothing enforces the rule. EDITOR-PAGE-OBJECTS moved the two EDITOR-58 reported; a scoped lint rule or a narrower sentence is proposed.
- `2026-10-30-uninstall-said-there-was-nothing-to-uninstall-before-planning-and-then-removed-a-projects-ejected-copies` — in a project whose config declares nothing, `uninstall` said "nothing to uninstall in this project" before planning, then removed the ejected copies and marker-stamped sub-agent files it found; the line now prints only where the plan is empty (2026-10-30). Resolved.
- `2026-10-30-a-reworded-success-line-left-a-negative-guarding-the-old-wording` — `update`'s success line was reworded when it began updating plugins; `update.e2e.test.ts` still guards a failed run against the retired line (`STEP_TEXT.UPDATE_PLUGINS_UNCHANGED`), so it cannot fail. Second case of the 2026-10-20 no-subject-negative class; its proposed standard never landed. CLI-918. The negatives and the constant they read are deleted (2026-10-31). Resolved.
- `2026-10-30-the-global-line-asked-the-roster-whether-home-holds-an-installation-and-the-roster-counts-an-empty-config-the-commands-do-not` — under a HOME holding only a config that declares nothing, a project config declaring nothing made compile, share, edit and list say "works on your global installation", then "No installation found": the line read HOME through the roster, which counts an empty config, and the commands through their own read, which does not. The line now asks whether HOME's config declares content (2026-10-30). Resolved.
- `2026-10-30-init-from-copied-skills-and-registered-the-project-at-home-before-saving-its-config-and-edit-orders-its-writes-the-same-way` — `init --from` that cannot write the project config (EACCES) leaves its copies and the home registration (and a global skill's copy and entry) with no project config; the project pair is written last, after the global pair, in `writeFromProjectContext`. `edit` copies and removes before its save too (read, not reproduced). Missing standard on write order. Partial: the init half landed (project pair first, a refused save removes the skill folders the run made); edit, plugins and the standard remain; CLI-982.
- `2026-10-30-the-entries-a-message-names-skills-by-were-indexed-in-two-places-and-six-messages-printed-ids` — six messages (carried skills written, unassigned skill, blocked assignment, project scope at home, share ejected, share left behind) printed skill ids; each now names by the recorded name, then the catalogue, then the id, from one index `namedByActiveEntries`, which the two inline copies now call.
- `2026-10-30-a-cancel-that-had-already-said-so-ended-through-this-error-and-oclif-said-it-again` — a declined `edit --from` printed "Edit cancelled" then " › Error: Cancelled"; a cancel that has said so ends through `this.exit(EXIT_CODES.CANCELLED)`
- `2026-10-30-a-marketplace-name-was-filled-from-another-config-apart-from-the-marketplace-it-labels` — a public-catalogue project under a custom-marketplace global was written with the global's marketplaceName (compile's writer fills it field by field), and `init --from` read the global's marketplace for an id naming none
- `2026-10-30-a-compile-spec-checked-stdout-for-a-warning-written-to-stderr-so-its-negative-could-never-fail` — `compile.test.ts` asserted a `warn()` line absent over stdout + error only, while `warn()` writes to stderr, so the negative could never fail; it now reads all three streams beside a positive twin
- `2026-10-30-a-line-said-once-per-run-is-held-back-by-any-earlier-line-with-the-same-text-even-from-another-scope` — the logger's once-per-run memory matches text from every warn(), so a project scan's line could silence an identical global line; the skipped-folders line names HOME's folder from `~/`, the logger rule is proposed
- `2026-10-30-a-test-helper-reading-the-emitted-config-by-string-convention-went-quiet-rather-than-red-when-the-format-moved` — readGeneratedUnionMembers answered `[]` for a quote style it missed, turning callers' `not.toContain` vacuous; it now throws. config-round-trip.test.ts's import-stripping regex had gone dead with the suite green; the helper is deleted
- `2026-10-30-a-workaround-keeping-a-ruled-out-cells-pairs-out-of-the-tab-order-was-pinned-by-no-spec` — skill-cell.tsx's conditional spread keeps a ruled-out cell's pairs out of the Tab order and no editor spec pinned it, so deleting it stayed green; a proposed incompatibility.spec.ts test closes it. SegmentedItem, LatticeRow and CommandBlock share the primitive's tabIndex-before-spread trap
- `2026-10-30-a-plugin-taken-into-a-project-was-installed-under-the-projects-marketplace-rather-than-the-one-its-entry-names` — edit --from taking a global plugin from another marketplace into a project installs `<id>@<project marketplace>`, which Claude refuses (exit 5); mode-migrator builds the same way but was not reached, install-plugin-skills is reached by a mixed-marketplaces red; a seated skill copied in a project reads the wrong marketplace folder (ENOENT)
- `2026-10-30-a-primitive-setting-its-tab-order-before-the-props-spread-let-a-callers-undefined-undo-it` — ButtonGroupItem wrote its roving tabIndex before `{...props}`, so a caller's `tabIndex={undefined}` made every radio a Tab stop; it now merges with `??` and skill-cell.tsx passes a plain prop. SegmentedItem, LatticeRow and CommandBlock now merge the same way, and the rule is in packages/ui/CLAUDE.md. Resolved.
- `2026-10-30-the-resolvesource-specs-pin-a-project-marketplace-through-a-config-that-declares-nothing` — buildSourceConfig() writes no skills and no agents, so the resolveSource specs pinning "a project config naming a marketplace is read first" run on a config the 2026-10-24 ruling says is no installation; a CLAUDE.md Test Data rule (project installations via buildProjectConfig) is proposed, held for the owner's answer on whether such a config's marketplace is read
- `2026-10-30-claude-answers-the-install-of-a-plugin-it-already-holds-with-success-so-an-install-list-is-not-what-a-run-newly-installed` — `claude plugin install` exits 0 for a plugin Claude already holds at that scope (measured, Claude Code 2.1.314), so `PluginInstallResult.installed` is not what a run newly installed; undoing a run must read the registry before installing. The claude stub writes no registry, so specs write it by hand (`holdPluginsForTheUser`); a CLAUDE.md Data Integrity rule is proposed
- `2026-10-30-a-built-site-whose-search-index-entry-was-empty-passed-every-site-check` — a www build left dist/pagefind/pagefind-entry.json at 0 bytes, so every search failed and no site check noticed; check-search-index.ts now refuses it. Starlight ranks the box with its own options, so a bare pagefind.search() orders results differently; the check asserts membership only
- `2026-10-30-a-turbo-dry-run-built-by-editing-a-command-string-ran-the-real-suites-outside-the-dist-lock` — a `sed`+`eval` loop meant to add `--dry=json` dropped it, so the web, CLI and server e2e suites ran for real outside the dist lock and rebuilt `dist/` under another lane's locked run; turbo's dry JSON also lists passthrough args on dependency tasks that never receive them. Proposed: suite-task commands run inside the lock even when dry, built as argv with the flag asserted
- `2026-10-30-a-refusal-sending-the-user-to-a-file-named-it-relative-to-a-root-the-user-was-not-at` — the undeclared-category refusal printed a home skill's metadata.yaml relative to its scope root, so from a project it named a file that is not there; its specs pinned the relative path. Proposed: clean-code-standards.md §12.4, a message sending the user to a file names its absolute path
- `2026-10-30-this-exit-inside-a-try-whose-catch-calls-handleerror-prints-error-eexit` — oclif's `this.exit` throws, and `handleError` re-raises what it catches, so an exit reached inside a `handleError` catch prints `Error: EEXIT: <code>`; met printing a line after `init --from`'s refused-save error. Proposed: clean-code-standards.md § 3, print with `{ exit: false }`, then throw a `CLIError` flagged `skipOclifErrorHandling`, outside such a try
- `2026-10-30-an-accessibility-audit-at-one-window-size-cannot-see-a-scroller-that-overflows-only-in-a-shorter-one` — every a11y.spec.ts audit ran at 1600x1000, where the Install dialog's lists fit, so scrollable-region-focusable saw nothing while the lists were unreachable by keyboard at 768px and below; the install audit now runs at 1600x640 behind an `overflows` guard. Proposed: an audit of a scroller sets a window size where it overflows
- `2026-10-29-a-forced-click-asserting-nothing-happened-cannot-tell-a-disabled-control-from-an-enabled-no-op` — a ruled-out cell's ••• and switches were enabled no-ops behind a forced-click spec whose comment claimed the subtree was aria-disabled; pair every forced press with toBeDisabled() on the same locator
- `2026-10-30-a-focus-clearance-tested-only-for-never-hiding-can-over-scroll-unseen` — the scroll clearance was tested only for never hiding the focused control, so Tab inside the pinned bar scrolled the page about 400px with every spec green (EDITOR-93); proposed: a clearance guard is tested with its converse, a visible stop moves nothing. The bottom edge stays unheld
- `2026-10-29-the-owed-save-question-lists-the-editors-other-modals-by-hand-so-a-new-modal-outside-dialog-reopens-it-over-that-modal` — the owed-save rename dialog waits on a hand-written list of open modals; the skill-contents dialog (open = `previewSkillId` + catalog `external` lookup) was missing, so it opened over it (EDITOR-96, fixed). Proposes a rule or one shared `usePreviewedSkill()` so a new modal or a changed derivation cannot drift. Open
- `2026-10-29-a-fixture-sat-at-a-machine-wide-path-on-the-premise-that-global-setup-cannot-hand-workers-a-location` — the e2e shared fixtures sat at one machine-wide folder, so two runs at once wiped each other's; the premise that global setup cannot hand workers a location was false. CLI-994 gives each run its own folder (resolved). Proposed: a fixture shared across a run's workers lives in a folder of that run
- `2026-10-30-a-race-test-starting-both-sides-in-one-tick-passed-without-the-tolerance-it-was-named-for` — two sweeps started in one tick moved in step, so the race test passed with its ENOENT tolerance deleted; it now staggers sweeps until the first finishes (resolved). Proposed: a race test shows its red against the code it names and staggers the sides
- `2026-10-31-a-note-called-four-layouts-hand-checked-while-unit-specs-pinned-them-and-its-absence-grep-could-not-fire-for-one` — the emission-scenarios.ts header said four config layouts were checked only by hand while config-types-source.test.ts and config-source.test.ts already pinned their bytes and formatting, and its `^import globalConfig` grep could never match a scenario constant; the note now names those specs and its grep is shown to fire (CLI-1003, resolved). Proposed: a "nothing checks X" sentence carries the census of the spec files, and a prints-nothing grep is run once on a file that holds the thing
- `2026-10-31-a-helper-the-compile-barrel-did-not-export-was-copied-into-the-cli-instead` — the "A, B and C" join lived in `@workspace/compile` and twice more in the CLI (`stacks-loader.ts`, `messages.ts`), copied because the compile barrel did not export it; the barrel now exports `inAnEnglishList`, `utils/string.ts` re-exports it and both copies are gone (resolved). Proposed: a helper the compile package declares but does not export is reached by exporting it, never by copying it
- `2026-10-31-a-declared-stacks-file-was-read-where-stacks-are-offered-and-defaulted-where-one-is-looked-up` — a marketplace's declared `stacksFile` was read when stacks were offered (`resolveOfferedStacks`) and defaulted when one was looked up (`loadStackById`), so `init --from` and `edit --from` refused its own stacks; the lookup now reads it, doctor's stacks-file validation now reads it too. Proposed: a source's declared layout resolved once, every reader through it
- `2026-10-31-a-switch-back-after-a-loop-of-codex-adds-was-skipped-by-the-first-refused-add` — refreshing a local Codex marketplace re-adds each plugin, and the switch-back after the loop sat outside any `try`, so one refused add left every switched-off plugin re-added before it switched on, unnamed; the loop now switches those back off and rethrows, a failed switch-back travelling with the refusal and named by `update` (resolved). Proposed: an undo after a loop of fallible actions runs over the ones that landed, and its own failure travels with the original
- `2026-10-31-a-value-import-of-the-compile-package-on-the-editors-first-paint-graph-breaks-the-budget` — swapping `agent-switches.ts`'s private "a, b and c" join for `@workspace/compile`'s put the whole compile chunk on the editor's first paint (513.0 KB against the 352.0 KB budget), and `spa-fallback-shell` hid the budget error behind "no index.html"; the copy stays, `provider.ts`'s "or" join is renamed `inAnEnglishAlternative` (open). Proposed: a first-paint editor module reaches a compile value only through a subpath export with its own chunk group, else keeps a local copy
- `2026-10-31-the-shells-grep-is-ugrep-reading-gitignore-so-a-census-from-the-root-skips-the-cli-guide` — in Claude Code's Bash, `grep` is ugrep with `--ignore-files`, and run from the repository root it skips `packages/cli/CLAUDE.md`, which the root `.gitignore` ignores and then re-includes with a `!` line, so a census of the briefing contract's links missed two of the three and showed no sign of it; `command grep` and `git grep` list it. Proposed: censuses over tracked files use `git grep` (briefing.md rule 3)
- `2026-10-31-a-vite-plugin-that-throws-in-closebundle-replaces-a-failed-builds-own-error` — `spa-fallback-shell` copied `index.html` in `closeBundle` and threw when it was missing; Vite 8.2 closes the bundle in a `finally`, so a failed build (the first-paint budget) printed only "no index.html", and the hook's `error` argument never carries a `generateBundle` failure; the copy now runs in `writeBundle`, which runs only after a successful write (resolved). Proposed: a build plugin fails the build from a hook that runs only on the path it judges, never from `closeBundle`
- `2026-10-31-a-test-outside-its-workspaces-vitest-include-never-runs-and-nothing-says-so` — `apps/editor/vitest.config.ts` extended the shared node config, which collects only `src/**/*.test.ts`, so `scripts/spa-fallback-shell.test.ts` never ran in the editor test gate and the gate said nothing; the config now adds `scripts/**/*.test.ts` (resolved: `bun run deps:check` refuses it through `check-vitest-collects-every-test.ts`). Proposed: a `deps:check` step that refuses a workspace test file outside `e2e/` its Vitest config does not collect
- `2026-10-31-a-plain-error-thrown-into-a-catch-that-reads-types-is-filed-against-the-fallbacks-file` — `loadStacks` threw a plain `Error` into the marketplace load whose catch (`matrixLoadFailure` in `source-validator.ts`) tells failures apart by type, so `doctor` filed a broken stacks file (and a schema-refused `config/skill-rules.ts`) against `config/skill-categories.ts` as "failed to load categories/rules"; `loadStacks` now throws `StacksLoadError` and both are filed against their own file (partial: `refuseCatalogueCollisions` still falls through). Proposed: a loader whose caller tells failures apart throws a typed error, never a plain `Error`, with a path field where one type covers several files
- `2026-10-31-a-path-built-from-os-tmpdir-differs-from-the-real-one-on-a-mac-and-no-linux-run-shows-it` — `check-vitest-collects-every-test.ts` compared paths joined onto the root as given with the real paths Vitest reports, so a root reached through a symlink (a Mac's `os.tmpdir()`) refused collected files and a relative `--root` crashed; `check()` now takes `realpathSync(path.resolve(...))` (resolved: `createTempDir` hands out the real path and the default test home is made through it, so the unit suite passes under a symlinked TMPDIR). Proposed: a spec takes the real path before comparing a path built under `os.tmpdir()` with one a tool reports, and a gate runs the unit suite once through a symlinked TMPDIR
- `2026-10-31-a-copy-result-lists-carried-skills-recorded-in-place-so-a-count-of-copies-counts-them` — `copyLocalSkills` returns a carried skill among its copies (recorded in place, `local: true`), so `init --from`'s refused-save line "Removed the N skills it copied" counted carried skills too; the refused save now counts them apart and says so in a second line (partial: the success-path "Copied N skills" counts in `init` and `edit` still include them, an owner question). Proposed: a count of copies read off `SkillCopyResult` filters in-place records first, or they move to their own field
- `2026-10-31-a-check-added-to-deps-check-reads-a-file-kind-the-git-hooks-do-not-trigger-on` — `check-vitest-collects-every-test.ts` joined `deps:check` and judges every test file, but `.husky/pre-commit` and `.husky/pre-push` ran `deps:check` only for manifests and shared-tool configs, so a commit or push adding only an uncollected test passed locally and only CI refused it; both `run_deps` regexes now match `*.test.ts(x)` and `pre-push-hook.test.ts` and `pre-commit-hook.test.ts` pin it (partial: the proposed standard is written nowhere, and nothing checks the trigger covers what each `deps:check` step reads). Proposed: a new `deps:check` step adds its file kinds to both hooks and a `pre-push-hook.test.ts` row in the same change
- `2026-10-31-captureoutput-drops-a-write-callback-passed-second-so-an-edit-run-waiting-on-one-hangs` — `@oclif/test`'s `captureOutput` answers a write callback only in third position, so `edit --from` run from source at a terminal hung to the 10s timeout under it; capture with a spy answering either position (as `edit-from-plugins-says-what-it-did.test.ts` does)
- `2026-10-31-a-bare-catch-around-a-codex-refresh-turned-a-plugin-left-switched-on-into-the-cached-version-warning` — `ensureMarketplace` wrapped the Codex refresh in a bare `catch {}`, so a plugin the user switched off that the refresh switched back on and could not switch off again was reported by `init --from` and `edit --from` as "continuing with cached version" with exit 0; the catch now tells the refresh's errors apart, warns each plugin by name with the line to set, and the run ends with `update`'s error and exit 1. Proposed: CLAUDE.md's visit-every-call-site rule also covers adding an error class that means partial success
- `2026-10-31-an-assertion-over-output-holding-the-temp-path-holds-only-while-the-temp-path-is-short-and-under-no-claude-folder` — specs asserting on output that holds a temp path held only for a short TMPDIR under no `.claude` folder: a path longer than the line is broken mid-word, so `flattenCliOutput` misses it, and a `.claude` negative over the whole output trips on the temp path itself; 3 unit specs and 7 e2e specs fixed, the e2e negatives through `compactedWithoutTempFolder` (resolved). Proposed: compare such output through `compactCliOutput`, and ask a folder-name negative of the path relative to the project or home, never of the whole output.
- `2026-10-31-an-env-var-a-ci-step-sets-for-a-turbo-command-never-reaches-the-task` — turbo 2's default strict env mode hands a task only the variables `turbo.json` declares, so `TMPDIR` set on check-cli's `bun run test --filter=agents-inc` step never reached vitest and the step stayed green against a mutant; the step now passes `--env-mode=loose`, shown red against that mutant and green unmutated
